cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/css/blender.css << 'CSS'
/* Blender-style CSS для UCH Knowledge Graph */
* {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    font-family: 'Segoe UI', Arial, sans-serif;
}

body {
    background: #0a0a1a;
    color: #ddd;
    overflow: hidden;
    height: 100vh;
}

/* Main 3D Viewport */
#viewport {
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    z-index: 1;
}

canvas {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
}

/* Blender-style panels */
.panel {
    position: fixed;
    background: rgba(25, 28, 35, 0.9);
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
    border: 1px solid #3a3f4a;
    border-radius: 4px;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
    z-index: 10;
    color: #ddd;
    font-size: 12px;
    pointer-events: auto;
    min-width: 200px;
    transition: opacity 0.2s, width 0.2s;
}

.panel-header {
    background: #2a2e37;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #3a3f4a;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 11px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
    color: #aaa;
    cursor: move;
    border-radius: 4px 4px 0 0;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    user-select: none;
}

.panel-header .arrow {
    transition: transform 0.3s;
    font-size: 10px;
    color: #667eea;
}

.panel-header.collapsed .arrow {
    transform: rotate(-90deg);
}

.panel-content {
    padding: 12px;
    transition: max-height 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease, padding 0.3s ease;
    max-height: 600px;
    opacity: 1;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.panel-header.collapsed + .panel-content {
    max-height: 0;
    opacity: 0;
    padding: 0 12px;
}

/* Top toolbar */
#toolbar {
    top: 10px;
    left: 50%;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    width: auto;
    min-width: 300px;
    background: rgba(30, 33, 40, 0.95);
}

.toolbar-buttons {
    display: flex;
    gap: 4px;
    padding: 5px;
}

.tool-btn {
    background: #353a45;
    border: 1px solid #4a4f5a;
    color: #ddd;
    padding: 6px 12px;
    border-radius: 3px;
    font-size: 11px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
}

.tool-btn:hover {
    background: #454a57;
    border-color: #667eea;
}

.tool-btn.active {
    background: #667eea;
    border-color: #8898ff;
    color: white;
}

/* Left panel - statistics */
#left-panel {
    top: 80px;
    left: 10px;
    width: 240px;
}

.stat-row {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    margin: 6px 0;
    padding: 4px 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #3a3f4a;
}

.stat-label {
    color: #999;
}

.stat-value {
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #ffd966;
}

.stat-highlight {
    font-size: 1.2em;
    color: #667eea;
    text-align: center;
    margin: 10px 0;
}

/* Right panel - controls */
#right-panel {
    top: 80px;
    right: 10px;
    width: 280px;
}

.control-section {
    margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.control-title {
    color: #aaa;
    font-size: 10px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    margin-bottom: 8px;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.control-item {
    margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.control-label {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    margin-bottom: 4px;
    color: #bbb;
}

.control-value {
    color: #667eea;
}

input[type=range] {
    -webkit-appearance: none;
    width: 100%;
    background: transparent;
}

input[type=range]::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
    width: 100%;
    height: 4px;
    background: #2a2e37;
    border-radius: 2px;
}

input[type=range]::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    -webkit-appearance: none;
    width: 14px;
    height: 14px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: #667eea;
    margin-top: -5px;
    cursor: pointer;
}

.color-control {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.color-label {
    width: 80px;
    color: #bbb;
}

.color-input {
    flex: 1;
    height: 22px;
    background: #2a2e37;
    border: 1px solid #4a4f5a;
    border-radius: 3px;
    cursor: pointer;
}

button {
    background: #353a45;
    border: 1px solid #4a4f5a;
    color: #ddd;
    padding: 6px 10px;
    border-radius: 3px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.2s;
    font-size: 11px;
}

button:hover {
    background: #454a57;
    border-color: #667eea;
}

/* Node info panel - шире чем controls */
#node-panel {
    bottom: 80px;
    right: 10px;
    width: 320px;
    max-height: 400px;
    display: none;
    flex-direction: column;
}

#node-panel.visible {
    display: flex;
}

#node-panel .panel-content {
    overflow-y: auto;
    max-height: 350px;
    padding: 12px;
}

.node-property {
    margin: 8px 0;
    padding: 6px 10px;
    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
    border-left: 2px solid #667eea;
    border-radius: 3px;
}

.node-property .label {
    color: #667eea;
    font-size: 10px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
}

.connection-item {
    padding: 6px 8px;
    margin: 4px 0;
    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02);
    border-left: 2px solid #ffaa00;
    border-radius: 3px;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 11px;
    transition: background 0.2s;
}

.connection-item:hover {
    background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

/* Bottom status bar - по правому краю */
#status-bar {
    bottom: 10px;
    right: 10px;
    width: auto;
    min-width: 300px;
    padding: 8px 15px;
    background: rgba(20, 22, 28, 0.95);
    display: flex;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    gap: 20px;
    font-size: 11px;
    color: #aaa;
    transform: none;
}

.status-item {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
}

.status-dot {
    width: 8px;
    height: 8px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: #4caf50;
}

.status-dot.warning {
    background: #ff9800;
}

/* Close button в стиле arrow */
.close-btn {
    background: none;
    border: none;
    color: #667eea;
    font-size: 14px;
    cursor: pointer;
    padding: 0 4px;
    transition: color 0.2s;
}

.close-btn:hover {
    color: #8898ff;
}

/* Размер узлов - динамический */
.node-size-1 { r: 2; }
.node-size-2 { r: 3; }
.node-size-3 { r: 4; }
.node-size-4 { r: 5; }

/* Scrollbar */
::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
    background: #2a2e37;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background: #4a4f5a;
    border-radius: 3px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
    background: #667eea;
}
CSS

# Создадим JS файл с конфигурацией
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/blender.js << 'JS'
// Конфигурация графа
const CONFIG = {
    linkWidth: {
        default: 0.65,
        min: 0,
        max: 2,
        step: 0.05
    },
    linkOpacity: {
        default: 0.45,
        min: 0,
        max: 1,
        step: 0.01
    },
    colors: {
        'Person': '#3366ff',
        'Technology': '#33cc33',
        'Component': '#ff9933',
        'Task': '#ffcc00',
        'Decision': '#cc33ff',
        'Chunk': '#666666'
    },
    nodeSizes: {
        base: 2.5,
        multiplier: 1
    },
    autoRotate: {
        speed: 0.00015,
        delay: 10000
    }
};

// Глобальные переменные
let graph;
let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
let lastInteraction = Date.now();
let fixedRadius = null;
let rotationAngle = 0;
let isRotating = false;
let rotationEnabled = true;
let selectedNodeId = null;
let cameraState = null;

// Цвета
const colors = { ...CONFIG.colors };

// Загрузка данных
fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => {
        if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
        
        const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
            let group = node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
            return {
                id: String(node.id),
                name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                group: group,
                val: 1
            };
        });
        
        const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
            source: String(edge.source),
            target: String(edge.target),
            type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
        }));
        
        const validLinks = links.filter(link => {
            return nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                   nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target);
        });
        
        graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
        initGraph();
        updateStats();
        initColorControls();
    })
    .catch(error => {
        console.error('Error loading data:', error);
        // Demo data
        graphData = {
            nodes: [
                { id: '1', name: 'Node 1', group: 'Person' },
                { id: '2', name: 'Node 2', group: 'Technology' },
                { id: '3', name: 'Node 3', group: 'Component' }
            ],
            links: [
                { source: '1', target: '2', type: 'knows' },
                { source: '2', target: '3', type: 'uses' }
            ]
        };
        initGraph();
    });

function initGraph() {
    const oldCanvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
    if (oldCanvas) oldCanvas.remove();
    
    graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
        .graphData(graphData)
        .nodeId('id')
        .nodeLabel('name')
        .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
        .nodeRelSize(CONFIG.nodeSizes.base)
        .linkWidth(CONFIG.linkWidth.default)
        .linkOpacity(CONFIG.linkOpacity.default)
        .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
        .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
        .onNodeHover(node => {
            document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
            if (!selectedNodeId) highlightNode(node);
        })
        .onNodeClick(node => {
            selectedNodeId = node.id;
            showNodeInfo(node);
            highlightNode(node, true);
        })
        .cooldownTime(2000);
    
    // Начальная позиция камеры
    graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
    
    // Сохраняем состояние камеры
    const camera = graph.camera();
    if (camera) {
        cameraState = {
            x: camera.position.x,
            y: camera.position.y,
            z: camera.position.z
        };
    }
    
    addStars();
    setupControls();
    
    // Запускаем авто-вращение
    if (rotationEnabled) {
        setupAutoRotate();
    }
}

function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
    if (!graph) return;
    
    if (node) {
        const nodeId = node.id;
        const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
            (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
            (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
        );
        
        const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
        connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
            connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
            connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
        });
        
        graph.nodeColor(n => {
            if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                return colors[n.group] || '#888';
            }
            return '#333';
        });
        
        graph.linkColor(l => {
            if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
            return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
        });
        
        graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : CONFIG.linkWidth.default);
    } else if (!permanent) {
        graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
             .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
             .linkWidth(CONFIG.linkWidth.default);
    }
}

function showNodeInfo(node) {
    const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
    const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
    const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
    const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#right-panel .panel-header');
    
    // Сворачиваем панель управления
    if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
        controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
    }
    
    panel.classList.add('visible');
    title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
    
    const connections = [];
    graphData.links.forEach(link => {
        const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
        const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
        
        if (sourceId === node.id) {
            const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
            connections.push({
                type: link.type || 'related',
                with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                direction: '→',
                targetId: targetId
            });
        }
        if (targetId === node.id) {
            const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
            connections.push({
                type: link.type || 'related',
                with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                direction: '←',
                targetId: sourceId
            });
        }
    });
    
    let html = `
        <div class="node-property">
            <div class="label">TYPE</div>
            <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="node-property">
            <div class="label">ID</div>
            <div>${node.id}</div>
        </div>
    `;
    
    if (connections.length > 0) {
        html += '<div style="margin-top: 10px;"><div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>';
        connections.forEach(conn => {
            html += `
                <div class="connection-item" onclick="focusNode('${conn.targetId}')">
                    ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                </div>
            `;
        });
        html += '</div>';
    }
    
    content.innerHTML = html;
}

function closeNodePanel() {
    const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
    panel.classList.remove('visible');
    selectedNodeId = null;
    highlightNode(null);
}

function focusNode(nodeId) {
    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
    if (node) {
        selectedNodeId = nodeId;
        showNodeInfo(node);
        highlightNode(node, true);
        
        // Плавное перемещение камеры
        if (graph) {
            const distance = 80;
            graph.cameraPosition(
                { x: distance, y: distance/2, z: distance },
                node,
                800
            );
            
            // Обновляем состояние камеры для авто-вращения
            const camera = graph.camera();
            if (camera) {
                fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                    camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                    camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                    camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                );
                rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
            }
        }
    }
}

function updateStats() {
    const groups = {};
    graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
    
    document.getElementById('total-nodes').textContent = graphData.nodes.length;
    document.getElementById('total-edges').textContent = graphData.links.length;
    
    document.getElementById('type-person').textContent = groups['Person'] || 0;
    document.getElementById('type-technology').textContent = groups['Technology'] || 0;
    document.getElementById('type-component').textContent = groups['Component'] || 0;
    document.getElementById('type-decision').textContent = groups['Decision'] || 0;
    document.getElementById('type-chunk').textContent = groups['Chunk'] || 0;
    
    // Search stats
    fetch('http://localhost:8003/stats')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            document.getElementById('text-chunks').textContent = data.total_chunks || 7654;
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    
    document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
}

function initColorControls() {
    const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
    if (!container) return;
    
    const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
    let html = '';
    
    groups.forEach(group => {
        const color = colors[group] || '#888';
        html += `
            <div class="color-control">
                <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${color}" 
                       onchange="updateGroupColor('${group}', this.value)">
            </div>
        `;
    });
    
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function updateGroupColor(group, color) {
    colors[group] = color;
    if (graph) {
        graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
        if (selectedNodeId) {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
            if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
        }
    }
}

function randomizeColors() {
    const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
    groups.forEach(group => {
        if (group !== 'Chunk') { // Chunk оставляем серым
            const randomColor = '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16);
            colors[group] = randomColor;
        }
    });
    
    initColorControls();
    if (graph) {
        graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
        if (selectedNodeId) {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
            if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
        }
    }
}

function resetColors() {
    Object.assign(colors, CONFIG.colors);
    initColorControls();
    if (graph) {
        graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
        if (selectedNodeId) {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
            if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
        }
    }
}

function setupControls() {
    // Link width
    const widthSlider = document.getElementById('link-width-slider');
    widthSlider.min = CONFIG.linkWidth.min;
    widthSlider.max = CONFIG.linkWidth.max;
    widthSlider.step = CONFIG.linkWidth.step;
    widthSlider.value = CONFIG.linkWidth.default;
    document.getElementById('link-width-value').textContent = CONFIG.linkWidth.default;
    widthSlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        document.getElementById('link-width-value').textContent = e.target.value;
        if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(e.target.value));
    });
    
    // Link opacity
    const opacitySlider = document.getElementById('link-opacity-slider');
    opacitySlider.min = CONFIG.linkOpacity.min;
    opacitySlider.max = CONFIG.linkOpacity.max;
    opacitySlider.step = CONFIG.linkOpacity.step;
    opacitySlider.value = CONFIG.linkOpacity.default;
    document.getElementById('link-opacity-value').textContent = CONFIG.linkOpacity.default;
    opacitySlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        document.getElementById('link-opacity-value').textContent = e.target.value;
        if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(e.target.value));
    });
    
    // Search
    document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => searchNodes(e.target.value));
    
    // Rotation toggle
    document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', toggleRotation);
}

function toggleRotation() {
    rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
    const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
    btn.textContent = rotationEnabled ? '⏸️ Stop' : '▶️ Start';
    
    if (rotationEnabled) {
        setupAutoRotate();
    } else {
        isRotating = false;
    }
}

function searchNodes(query) {
    if (!query || !graphData.nodes) return;
    
    const results = graphData.nodes.filter(n => 
        n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
    ).slice(0, 5);
    
    const container = document.getElementById('search-results');
    container.innerHTML = results.map(r => 
        `<button style="font-size:10px; padding:2px 4px;" onclick="focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`
    ).join('');
}

function setupAutoRotate() {
    let lastTime = Date.now();
    
    function rotateCamera() {
        if (!graph || !rotationEnabled) return;
        
        const now = Date.now();
        const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
        lastTime = now;
        
        if (now - lastInteraction > CONFIG.autoRotate.delay) {
            if (!isRotating) {
                isRotating = true;
                const camera = graph.camera();
                if (camera) {
                    fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                        camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                        camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                        camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                    );
                    rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                }
            }
            
            if (isRotating && fixedRadius) {
                rotationAngle += deltaTime * CONFIG.autoRotate.speed;
                const camera = graph.camera();
                if (camera) {
                    camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                    camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                }
            }
        } else {
            isRotating = false;
        }
        
        requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
    }
    
    // Track user interaction
    const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
    if (canvas) {
        canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
        canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
    }
    
    rotateCamera();
}

function addStars() {
    const scene = graph.scene();
    if (!scene) return;
    
    function createStars(count, radius, color, size, opacity) {
        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
            positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
            positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
        }
        
        geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
        
        const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            color, size, transparent: true, opacity,
            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
        });
        
        return new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
    }
    
    scene.add(createStars(4000, 800, 0xffffff, 0.3, 0.8));
    scene.add(createStars(2000, 1200, 0xaaccff, 0.2, 0.6));
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a1a, 0.0003);
}

// Make panels draggable
document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
    let panel = header.parentElement;
    let isDragging = false;
    let offsetX, offsetY;
    
    header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        if (e.target === header || e.target.parentElement === header) {
            isDragging = true;
            offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
            offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
            panel.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        }
    });
    
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        if (isDragging) {
            panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
            panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
            panel.style.transform = 'none';
        }
    });
    
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
        isDragging = false;
        panel.style.cursor = 'default';
    });
});

// Collapsible panels with node panel logic
document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
    header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
            const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
            header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
            
            // Если это панель управления и мы её разворачиваем
            if (header.parentElement.id === 'right-panel' && wasCollapsed) {
                closeNodePanel();
            }
        }
    });
});

        <div id="controls-content">

            <div style="margin-top: 10px;">Link opacity: <span id="opacity-value">0.45</span></div>
            <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
            
            <button id="rotate-btn" style="margin-top: 10px; width:100%;">⏸️ Stop rotation</button>
        </div>

        const debug = document.getElementById('debug-content');
        let graph;
        let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        let rotationEnabled = true;
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        
        function log(msg) {
            console.log(msg);
            debug.innerHTML += new Date().toLocaleTimeString() + ': ' + msg + '<br>';
            debug.scrollTop = debug.scrollHeight;
        }
        
        log('Starting...');
        
        // Загружаем данные
        log('Fetching data from http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200');
        
        fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
            .then(res => {

                return res.json();
            })
            .then(data => {
                log('Data received');
                log('Nodes: ' + (data.nodes ? data.nodes.length : 0));
                log('Edges: ' + (data.edges ? data.edges.length : 0));
                
                if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) {
                    log('ERROR: No nodes or edges in data');
                    return;
                }
                
                // Преобразуем данные
                const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                    return {
                        id: String(node.id),
                        name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                        group: node.group || node.type || 'unknown'
                    };
                });
                
                const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
                    source: String(edge.source),
                    target: String(edge.target),
                    type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
                }));
                
                // Фильтруем валидные связи
                const validLinks = links.filter(link => {
                    return nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                           nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target);
                });
                
                graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
                log('Processed: ' + nodes.length + ' nodes, ' + validLinks.length + ' links');
                
                // Обновляем статистику
                updateStats();
                
                // Создаем граф
                createGraph();
            })
            .catch(error => {
                log('ERROR: ' + error.message);
            });
        
        function createGraph() {
            try {
                log('Creating graph...');
                
                graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
                    .graphData(graphData)
                    .nodeId('id')
                    .nodeLabel('name')
                    .nodeColor(n => getNodeColor(n.group))
                    .linkWidth(0.65)
                    .linkOpacity(0.45)
                    .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
                    .onNodeHover(node => {
                        document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
                    })
                    .onNodeClick(node => {
                        log('Clicked: ' + node.name);
                    });
                
                log('Graph created successfully');
                
                // Начальная позиция камеры
                graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
                
                // Добавляем звезды
                addStars();
                
                // Запускаем авто-вращение
                setupAutoRotate();
                
                // Настраиваем контролы
                setupControls();
                
            } catch (e) {
                log('Graph creation error: ' + e.message);
            }
        }
        
        function getNodeColor(group) {
            const colors = {
                'Person': '#3366ff',
                'Technology': '#33cc33',
                'Component': '#ff9933',
                'Decision': '#cc33ff',
                'Chunk': '#666666'
            };
            return colors[group] || '#888';
        }
        
        function updateStats() {
            const groups = {};
            graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
            
            let html = `
                <div class="stat-row"><span>Total nodes:</span> <span>${graphData.nodes.length}</span></div>
                <div class="stat-row"><span>Total edges:</span> <span>${graphData.links.length}</span></div>
            `;
            
            for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
                html += `<div class="stat-row"><span>${group}:</span> <span>${count}</span></div>`;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function setupControls() {
            // Link width
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            // Link opacity
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            // Rotation toggle
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                document.getElementById('rotate-btn').textContent = 
                    rotationEnabled ? '⏸️ Stop rotation' : '▶️ Start rotation';
            });
        }
        
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            let fixedRadius = null;
            let rotationAngle = 0;
            let isRotating = false;
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graph || !rotationEnabled) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                if (now - lastInteraction > 5000) {
                    if (!isRotating) {
                        isRotating = true;
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        if (camera) {
                            fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                                camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                                camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                                camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                            );
                            rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                        }
                    }
                    
                    if (isRotating && fixedRadius) {
                        rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        if (camera) {
                            camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                            camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                            camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    isRotating = false;
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
                canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        function addStars() {
            const scene = graph.scene();
            if (!scene) return;
            
            function createStars(count, radius, color, size, opacity) {
                const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
                const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
                
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                    positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                    positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                }
                
                geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
                
                const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
                    color, size, transparent: true, opacity,
                    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
                });
                
                return new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            }
            
            scene.add(createStars(2000, 500, 0xffffff, 0.2, 0.8));
            scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a1a, 0.001);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<body>
    <div id="viewport"></div>
    
    <!-- Statistics Panel -->
    <div id="stats-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>📊 STATISTICS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="stats-content">
            Loading...
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Controls Panel -->
    <div id="controls-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>🎮 CONTROLS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">APPEARANCE</div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link width</span>
                        <span id="width-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.65</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.65">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>
                        <span id="opacity-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.45</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button onclick="randomizeColors()">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button onclick="resetColors()">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">ROTATION</div>
                <button id="rotate-btn" style="width: 100%;">⏸️ Stop</button>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">SEARCH</div>
                <input type="text" id="search-input" placeholder="Search nodes...">
                <div id="search-results"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Node Info Panel -->
    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="close-btn" onclick="closeNodePanel()">✖</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
            Select a node to view details
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Status Panel -->
    <div id="status-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Graph: Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        // ========== ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЕ ПЕРЕМЕННЫЕ ==========
        let graph;
        let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        let colors = {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#666666'
        };
        let rotationEnabled = true;
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        let selectedNodeId = null;
        let fixedRadius = null;
        let rotationAngle = 0;
        
        // ========== ЗАГРУЗКА ДАННЫХ ==========
        fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
                
                const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => ({
                    id: String(node.id),
                    name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                    group: node.group || node.type || 'unknown'
                }));
                
                const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
                    source: String(edge.source),
                    target: String(edge.target),
                    type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
                }));
                
                const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
                );
                
                graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
                initGraph();
                updateStats();
                initColorControls();
            });
        
        // ========== ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ГРАФА ==========
        function initGraph() {
            graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
                .graphData(graphData)
                .nodeId('id')
                .nodeLabel('name')
                .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                .linkWidth(0.65)
                .linkOpacity(0.45)
                .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
                .onNodeHover(node => {
                    document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
                    if (!selectedNodeId) highlightNode(node);
                })
                .onNodeClick(node => {
                    selectedNodeId = node.id;
                    showNodeInfo(node);
                    highlightNode(node, true);
                    
                    // Сворачиваем controls при открытии node info
                    const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                    if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                        controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                    }
                });
            
            graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
            addStars();
            setupControls();
            setupAutoRotate();
        }
        
        // ========== ПОДСВЕТКА УЗЛОВ ==========
        function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
            if (!graph) return;
            
            if (node) {
                const nodeId = node.id;
                const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
                    (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
                    (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
                );
                
                const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
                connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
                });
                
                graph.nodeColor(n => {
                    if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                        return colors[n.group] || '#888';
                    }
                    return '#333';
                });
                
                graph.linkColor(l => {
                    if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
                    return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                });
                
                graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : 0.65);
            } else if (!permanent) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                     .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
                     .linkWidth(0.65);
            }
        }
        
        // ========== ПАНЕЛЬ ИНФОРМАЦИИ ОБ УЗЛЕ ==========
        function showNodeInfo(node) {
            const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
            const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
            
            panel.classList.add('visible');
            title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
            
            const connections = [];
            graphData.links.forEach(link => {
                const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
                const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
                
                if (sourceId === node.id) {
                    const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                        direction: '→',
                        targetId: targetId
                    });
                }
                if (targetId === node.id) {
                    const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                        direction: '←',
                        targetId: sourceId
                    });
                }
            });
            
            let html = `
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                    <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">ID</div>
                    <div>${node.id}</div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            if (connections.length > 0) {
                html += '<div style="margin-top: 10px;"><div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>';
                connections.forEach(conn => {
                    html += `
                        <div class="connection-item" onclick="focusNode('${conn.targetId}')">
                            ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
                html += '</div>';
            }
            
            content.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function closeNodePanel() {
            document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
            selectedNodeId = null;
            highlightNode(null);
        }
        
        function focusNode(nodeId) {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (node) {
                selectedNodeId = nodeId;
                showNodeInfo(node);
                highlightNode(node, true);
                
                if (graph) {
                    graph.cameraPosition(
                        { x: 80, y: 40, z: 80 },
                        node,
                        800
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        
        // ========== СТАТИСТИКА ==========
        function updateStats() {
            const groups = {};
            graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
            
            let html = `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.nodes.length}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.links.length}</span>
                </div>
            `;
            
            for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
                html += `
                    <div class="stat-row">
                        <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                        <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
            document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
        }
        
        // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ЦВЕТАМИ ==========
        function initColorControls() {
            const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
            if (!container) return;
            
            const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
            let html = '';
            
            groups.forEach(group => {
                if (group !== 'unknown') {
                    html += `
                        <div class="color-control">
                            <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                            <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${colors[group] || '#888'}" 
                                   onchange="updateGroupColor('${group}', this.value)">
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
            });
            
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function updateGroupColor(group, color) {
            colors[group] = color;
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        function randomizeColors() {
            const groups = Object.keys(colors);
            groups.forEach(group => {
                if (group !== 'Chunk') {
                    colors[group] = '#' + Math.floor(Math.random()*16777215).toString(16);
                }
            });
            initColorControls();
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        function resetColors() {
            colors = {
                'Person': '#3366ff',
                'Technology': '#33cc33',
                'Component': '#ff9933',
                'Decision': '#cc33ff',
                'Chunk': '#666666'
            };
            initColorControls();
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ==========
        function setupControls() {
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                document.getElementById('rotate-btn').textContent = rotationEnabled ? '⏸️ Stop' : '▶️ Start';
            });
            
            document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
                if (!query) {
                    document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                    return;
                }
                
                const results = graphData.nodes
                    .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                    .slice(0, 5);
                
                document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = results
                    .map(r => `<button onclick="focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`)
                    .join('');
            });
        }
        
        // ========== АВТО-ВРАЩЕНИЕ ==========
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graph || !rotationEnabled) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                if (now - lastInteraction > 5000) {
                    if (!fixedRadius) {
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                            camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                            camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                            camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                        );
                        rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                    }
                    
                    rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                    camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                } else {
                    fixedRadius = null;
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
                canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        // ========== ЗВЕЗДЫ ==========
        function addStars() {
            const scene = graph.scene();
            if (!scene) return;
            
            function createStars(count, radius) {
                const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
                const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
                
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                    positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                    positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                }
                
                geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
                
                const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
                    color: 0xffffff, 
                    size: 0.2,
                    transparent: true,
                    opacity: 0.8,
                    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
                });
                
                return new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            }
            
            scene.add(createStars(2000, 400));
        }
        
        // ========== DRAGGABLE PANELS ==========
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            let panel = header.parentElement;
            let isDragging = false;
            let offsetX, offsetY;
            
            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    isDragging = true;
                    offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                    offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDragging) {
                    panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                    panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDragging = false;
            });
        });
        
        // ========== COLLAPSIBLE PANELS ==========
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
                    header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                    
                    if (header.parentElement.id === 'controls-panel' && !wasCollapsed) {
                        closeNodePanel();
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<body>
    <div id="viewport"></div>
    
    <!-- Statistics Panel -->
    <div id="stats-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>📊 STATISTICS</span>

            <input type="text" id="search-input" placeholder="Search nodes...">
