```bash
    <!-- Controls Panel -->
    <div id="controls-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>🎮 CONTROLS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">APPEARANCE</div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link width</span>
                        <span id="width-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.65</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.65">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>
                        <span id="opacity-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.45</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Stars count</span>
                        <span id="stars-value" style="color: #667eea;">2000</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="stars-slider" min="0" max="4000" step="100" value="2000">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button onclick="randomizeColors()">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button onclick="resetColors()">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Node Info Panel -->
    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="close-btn" onclick="closeNodePanel()">✖</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
            Select a node to view details
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Bottom Panel -->
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
        <button id="rotate-btn" class="icon-btn">
            <span>⏸️</span>
            <span>Stop</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        // ========== ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЕ ПЕРЕМЕННЫЕ ==========
        let graph;
        let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        let colors = {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#666666'
        };
        let rotationEnabled = true;
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        let selectedNodeId = null;
        let fixedRadius = null;
        let rotationAngle = 0;
        let starsCount = 2000;
        let starsMesh = null;
        
        // ========== ЗАГРУЗКА ДАННЫХ ==========
        fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
                
                const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                    // Считаем количество связей для размера узла
                    const connections = data.edges.filter(e => 
                        e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
                    ).length;
                    
                    return {
                        id: String(node.id),
                        name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                        group: node.group || node.type || 'unknown',
                        val: Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.ceil(connections / 5))) // Размер от 1 до 5
                    };
                });
                
                const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
                    source: String(edge.source),
                    target: String(edge.target),
                    type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
                }));
                
                const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
                );
                
                graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
                initGraph();
                updateStats();
                initColorControls();
            });
        
        // ========== ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ГРАФА ==========
        function initGraph() {
            graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
                .graphData(graphData)
                .nodeId('id')
                .nodeLabel('name')
                .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                .nodeVal('val')  // Динамический размер
                .linkWidth(0.65)
                .linkOpacity(0.45)
                .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
                .onNodeHover(node => {
                    document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
                    if (!selectedNodeId) highlightNode(node);
                })
                .onNodeClick(node => {
                    selectedNodeId = node.id;
                    showNodeInfo(node);
                    highlightNode(node, true);
                    
                    // Сворачиваем controls и позиционируем node panel
                    const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                    if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                        controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                    }
                    
                    // Ждем анимацию сворачивания и позиционируем node panel
                    setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                })
                .onBackgroundClick(() => {
                    if (selectedNodeId) {
                        closeNodePanel();
                    }
                });
            
            graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
            addStars(starsCount);
            setupControls();
            setupAutoRotate();
        }
        
        // ========== ПОЗИЦИОНИРОВАНИЕ ПАНЕЛИ ИНФО ==========
        function positionNodePanel() {
            const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
            const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            
            if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel) return;
            
            const controlsRect = controlsPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
            const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsRect.height;
            
            nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
        }
        
        // ========== ПОДСВЕТКА УЗЛОВ ==========
        function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
            if (!graph) return;
            
            if (node) {
                const nodeId = node.id;
                const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
                    (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
                    (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
                );
                
                const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
                connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
                });
                
                graph.nodeColor(n => {
                    if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                        return colors[n.group] || '#888';
                    }
                    return '#333';
                });
                
                graph.linkColor(l => {
                    if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
                    return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                });
                
                graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : 0.65);
            } else if (!permanent) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                     .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
                     .linkWidth(0.65);
            }
        }
        
        // ========== ПАНЕЛЬ ИНФОРМАЦИИ ОБ УЗЛЕ ==========
        function showNodeInfo(node) {
            const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
            const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
            
            panel.classList.add('visible');
            title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
            
            const connections = [];
            graphData.links.forEach(link => {
                const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
                const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
                
                if (sourceId === node.id) {
                    const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                        direction: '→',
                        targetId: targetId
                    });
                }
                if (targetId === node.id) {
                    const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                        direction: '←',
                        targetId: sourceId
                    });
                }
            });
            
            let html = `
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                    <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">ID</div>
                    <div>${node.id}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>
                    <div>${connections.length}</div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            if (connections.length > 0) {
                connections.forEach(conn => {
                    html += `
                        <div class="connection-item" onclick="focusNode('${conn.targetId}')">
                            ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
            }
            
            content.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function closeNodePanel() {
            document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
            selectedNodeId = null;
            highlightNode(null);
        }
        
        function focusNode(nodeId) {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (node) {
                selectedNodeId = nodeId;
                showNodeInfo(node);
                highlightNode(node, true);
                
                // Сворачиваем controls
                const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                    controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                }
                
                setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                
                if (graph) {
                    graph.cameraPosition(
                        { x: 80, y: 40, z: 80 },
                        node,
                        800
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        
        // ========== СТАТИСТИКА ==========
        function updateStats() {
            const groups = {};
            graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
            
            let html = `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.nodes.length}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.links.length}</span>
                </div>
            `;
            
            for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
                html += `
                    <div class="stat-row">
                        <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                        <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
            document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
        }
        
        // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ЦВЕТАМИ ==========
        function initColorControls() {
            const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
            if (!container) return;
            
            const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
            let html = '';
            
            groups.forEach(group => {
                if (group !== 'unknown') {
                    html += `
                        <div class="color-control">
                            <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                            <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${colors[group] || '#888'}" 
                                   onchange="updateGroupColor('${group}', this.value)">
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
            });
            
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function updateGroupColor(group, color) {
            colors[group] = color;
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Функция для генерации ярких контрастных цветов
        function getBrightColor() {
            const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
            // Используем насыщенные цвета (saturation 70-100%, lightness 50-70%)
            return `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 60%)`;
        }
        
        function randomizeColors() {
            const groups = Object.keys(colors);
            groups.forEach(group => {
                colors[group] = getBrightColor();
            });
            initColorControls();
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        function resetColors() {
            colors = {
                'Person': '#3366ff',
                'Technology': '#33cc33',
                'Component': '#ff9933',
                'Decision': '#cc33ff',
                'Chunk': '#666666'
            };
            initColorControls();
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ==========
        function setupControls() {
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('stars-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                document.getElementById('stars-value').textContent = val;
                starsCount = val;
                updateStars(val);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
                btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? '<span>⏸️</span><span>Stop</span>' : '<span>▶️</span><span>Start</span>';
                
                if (rotationEnabled) {
                    // Возобновляем вращение с текущей позиции
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                        camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                        camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                        camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                    );
                    rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
                if (!query) {
                    document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                    return;
                }
                
                const results = graphData.nodes
                    .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                    .slice(0, 5);
                
                document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = results
                    .map(r => `<button onclick="focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`)
                    .join('');
            });
        }
        
        // ========== АВТО-ВРАЩЕНИЕ ==========
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graph) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                if (rotationEnabled && now - lastInteraction > 5000) {
                    if (!fixedRadius) {
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                            camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                            camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                            camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                        );
                        rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                    }
                    
                    rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                    camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                } else {
                    fixedRadius = null;
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
                canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        // ========== ЗВЕЗДЫ ==========
        function addStars(count) {
            const scene = graph.scene();
            if (!scene) return;
            
            // Удаляем старые звезды
            if (starsMesh) {
                scene.remove(starsMesh);
            }
            
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            const colors_array = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                // Позиции
                positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                
                // Цвета (немного вариации)
                const brightness = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3;
                colors_array[i*3] = brightness;
                colors_array[i*3+1] = brightness * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2);
                colors_array[i*3+2] = brightness;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors_array, 3));
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
                size: 0.3,
                vertexColors: true,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.8,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            starsMesh = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            scene.add(starsMesh);
        }
        
        function updateStars(count) {
            addStars(count);
        }
        
        // ========== DRAGGABLE PANELS ==========
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            let panel = header.parentElement;
            let isDragging = false;
            let offsetX, offsetY;
            
            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    isDragging = true;
                    offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                    offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDragging) {
                    panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                    panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                    
                    // Если перемещаем controls, обновляем позицию node panel
                    if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDragging = false;
            });
        });
        
        // ========== COLLAPSIBLE PANELS ==========
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
                    header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                    
                    if (header.parentElement.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        if (!wasCollapsed) {
                            // Разворачиваем controls - закрываем node panel
                            closeNodePanel();
                        } else {
                            // Сворачиваем controls - позиционируем node panel
                            setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
        
        // Следим за изменениями размеров окна
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            positionNodePanel();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

```bash
<body>
    <div id="viewport"></div>
    
    <div id="stats-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>📊 STATISTICS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>

    </div>

            <span>🔍 SEARCH</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>

            <input type="text" id="search-input" placeholder="Search nodes...">
            <div id="search-results"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

            <span>🎮 CONTROLS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <div class="control-section">

                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link width</span>
                        <span id="width-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.65</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.65">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>
                        <span id="opacity-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.45</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Stars count</span>
                        <span id="stars-value" style="color: #667eea;">2000</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="stars-slider" min="0" max="4000" step="100" value="2000">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button onclick="randomizeColors()">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button onclick="resetColors()">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="close-btn" onclick="closeNodePanel()">✖</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
            Select a node to view details
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
        <button id="rotate-btn" class="icon-btn">
            <span>⏸️</span>
            <span>Stop</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        // ========== ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЕ ПЕРЕМЕННЫЕ ==========
        let graph;
        let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        let colors = {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#292929'  // Изменено на #292929
        };
        let rotationEnabled = true;
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        let selectedNodeId = null;
        let fixedRadius = null;
        let rotationAngle = 0;
        let starsCount = 2000;
        let starsMesh = null;
        let isDraggingGraph = false;  // Флаг для отслеживания зажатой ЛКМ
        
        // ========== ЗАГРУЗКА ДАННЫХ ==========
        fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
                
                const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                    const group = node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
                    const connections = data.edges.filter(e => 
                        e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
                    ).length;
                    
                    // Размер узла: Chunk - 50% от обычного, остальные от 2 до 6
                    let val;
                    if (group === 'Chunk') {
                        val = Math.max(1, Math.min(3, Math.ceil(connections / 10))); // Chunk: 1-3
                    } else {
                        val = Math.max(2, Math.min(6, Math.ceil(connections / 3))); // Другие: 2-6
                    }
                    
                    return {
                        id: String(node.id),
                        name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                        group: group,
                        val: val
                    };
                });
                
                const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
                    source: String(edge.source),
                    target: String(edge.target),
                    type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
                }));
                
                const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
                );
                
                graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
                initGraph();
                updateStats();
                initColorControls();
            });
        
        // ========== ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ГРАФА ==========
        function initGraph() {
            graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
                .graphData(graphData)
                .nodeId('id')
                .nodeLabel('name')
                .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                .nodeVal('val')
                .linkWidth(0.65)
                .linkOpacity(0.45)
                .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
                .onNodeHover(node => {
                    document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
                    if (!selectedNodeId && !isDraggingGraph) highlightNode(node);
                })
                .onNodeClick(node => {
                    if (!isDraggingGraph) {
                        selectedNodeId = node.id;
                        showNodeInfo(node);
                        highlightNode(node, true);
                        
                        const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                        if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                            controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                        }
                        
                        setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                    }
                })
                .onBackgroundClick(() => {
                    if (selectedNodeId) {
                        closeNodePanel();
                    }
                });
            
            // Отслеживаем начало и конец перетаскивания графа
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                    isDraggingGraph = true;
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                    isDraggingGraph = false;
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                    isDraggingGraph = false;
                });
            }
            
            graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
            addStars(starsCount);
            setupControls();
            setupAutoRotate();
        }
        
        // ========== ПОЗИЦИОНИРОВАНИЕ ПАНЕЛИ ИНФО ==========
        function positionNodePanel() {
            const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
            const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            
            if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel) return;
            
            const controlsRect = controlsPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
            const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsRect.height;
            
            nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
        }
        
        // ========== ПОДСВЕТКА УЗЛОВ ==========
        function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
            if (!graph) return;
            
            if (node) {
                const nodeId = node.id;
                const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
                    (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
                    (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
                );
                
                const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
                connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
                });
                
                graph.nodeColor(n => {
                    if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                        return colors[n.group] || '#888';
                    }
                    return '#333';
                });
                
                graph.linkColor(l => {
                    if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
                    return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                });
                
                graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : 0.65);
            } else if (!permanent) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                     .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
                     .linkWidth(0.65);
            }
        }
        
        // ========== ПАНЕЛЬ ИНФОРМАЦИИ ОБ УЗЛЕ ==========
        function showNodeInfo(node) {
            const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
            const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
            
            panel.classList.add('visible');
            title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
            
            const connections = [];
            graphData.links.forEach(link => {
                const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
                const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
                
                if (sourceId === node.id) {
                    const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                        direction: '→',
                        targetId: targetId
                    });
                }
                if (targetId === node.id) {
                    const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                        direction: '←',
                        targetId: sourceId
                    });
                }
            });
            
            let html = `
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                    <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">ID</div>
                    <div>${node.id}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>
                    <div>${connections.length}</div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            if (connections.length > 0) {
                connections.forEach(conn => {
                    html += `
                        <div class="connection-item" onclick="focusNode('${conn.targetId}')">
                            ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
            }
            
            content.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function closeNodePanel() {
            document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
            selectedNodeId = null;
            highlightNode(null);
        }
        
        function focusNode(nodeId) {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (node) {
                selectedNodeId = nodeId;
                showNodeInfo(node);
                highlightNode(node, true);
                
                const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                    controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                }
                
                setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                
                if (graph) {
                    graph.cameraPosition(
                        { x: 80, y: 40, z: 80 },
                        node,
                        800
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        
        // ========== СТАТИСТИКА ==========
        function updateStats() {
            const groups = {};
            graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
            
            let html = `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.nodes.length}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.links.length}</span>
                </div>
            `;
            
            for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
                html += `
                    <div class="stat-row">
                        <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                        <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
            document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
        }
        
        // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ЦВЕТАМИ ==========
        function initColorControls() {
            const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
            if (!container) return;
            
            const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
            let html = '';
            
            groups.forEach(group => {
                if (group !== 'unknown') {
                    const color = colors[group] || (group === 'Chunk' ? '#292929' : '#888');
                    html += `
                        <div class="color-control">
                            <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                            <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${color}" 
                                   onchange="updateGroupColor('${group}', this.value)">
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
            });
            
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function updateGroupColor(group, color) {
            colors[group] = color;
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        function getBrightColor() {
            const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
            return `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 60%)`;
        }
        
        function randomizeColors() {
            const groups = Object.keys(colors);
            groups.forEach(group => {
                colors[group] = getBrightColor();
            });
            // Обновляем отображение в панели
            initColorControls();
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        function resetColors() {
            colors = {
                'Person': '#3366ff',
                'Technology': '#33cc33',
                'Component': '#ff9933',
                'Decision': '#cc33ff',
                'Chunk': '#292929'
            };
            initColorControls();
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ==========
        function setupControls() {
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('stars-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                document.getElementById('stars-value').textContent = val;
                starsCount = val;
                updateStars(val);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
                btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? '<span>⏸️</span><span>Stop</span>' : '<span>▶️</span><span>Start</span>';
                
                if (rotationEnabled) {
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                        camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                        camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                        camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                    );
                    rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
                if (!query) {
                    document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                    return;
                }
                
                const results = graphData.nodes
                    .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                    .slice(0, 5);
                
                document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = results
                    .map(r => `<button onclick="focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`)
                    .join('');
            });
        }
        
        // ========== АВТО-ВРАЩЕНИЕ ==========
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graph) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                if (rotationEnabled && !isDraggingGraph && now - lastInteraction > 5000) {
                    if (!fixedRadius) {
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                            camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                            camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                            camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                        );
                        rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                    }
                    
                    rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                    camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                } else {
                    fixedRadius = null;
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                    lastInteraction = Date.now();
                    isDraggingGraph = true;
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                    isDraggingGraph = false;
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        // ========== ЗВЕЗДЫ ==========
        function addStars(count) {
            const scene = graph.scene();
            if (!scene) return;
            
            if (starsMesh) {
                scene.remove(starsMesh);
            }
            
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            const colors_array = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                
                const brightness = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3;
                colors_array[i*3] = brightness;
                colors_array[i*3+1] = brightness * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2);
                colors_array[i*3+2] = brightness;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors_array, 3));
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
                size: 0.3,
                vertexColors: true,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.8,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            starsMesh = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            scene.add(starsMesh);
        }
        
        function updateStars(count) {
            addStars(count);
        }
        
        // ========== DRAGGABLE PANELS ==========
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            let panel = header.parentElement;
            let isDragging = false;
            let offsetX, offsetY;
            
            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    isDragging = true;
                    offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                    offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDragging) {
                    panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                    panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                    
                    if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDragging = false;
            });
        });
        
        // ========== COLLAPSIBLE PANELS ==========
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
                    header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                    
                    if (header.parentElement.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        if (!wasCollapsed) {
                            closeNodePanel();
                        } else {
                            setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
        
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            positionNodePanel();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## 👤 **Kirill**

2. Одиночное нажатие по-прежнему срабатывает как удержание нажатия

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Отличные замечания! Давай решать по порядку:

1. **Проблема большого HTML** - сделаем модульную структуру с загрузкой компонентов
2. **Масштаб графа** - уменьшим граф и добавим автоматическое масштабирование
3. **Нажатие мыши** - исправим event propagation
4. **Поиск** - поправим отступы

**ПЕРВАЯ КОМАНДА:**
```bash
# Создадим модульную структуру
mkdir -p /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/modules

# Создадим главный загрузчик
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/loader.js << 'EOF'
// Загрузчик модулей
async function loadModule(url) {
    const response = await fetch(url);
    const code = await response.text();
    const module = new Function('exports', 'require', 'module', '__filename', '__dirname', code);
    const exports = {};
    const module = { exports };
    module(exports, null, module);
    return module.exports;
}

// Загружаем все модули
async function loadAllModules() {
    const modules = {};
    
    // Загружаем компоненты
    const components = [
        'config',
        'graph-core',
        'colors',
        'controls',
        'panels',
        'stars',
        'utils'
    ];
    
    for (const comp of components) {
        try {
            modules[comp] = await loadModule(`js/modules/${comp}.js`);
            console.log(`✅ Loaded module: ${comp}`);
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn(`⚠️ Module ${comp} not loaded:`, e);
        }
    }
    
    return modules;
}
EOF

# Создадим конфиг
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/modules/config.js << 'EOF'
// Конфигурация графа
export default {
    graph: {
        scale: 0.5,  // Уменьшенный масштаб
        camera: {
            initial: { x: 110, y: 60, z: 150 },  // Половинный размер
            focus: { x: 40, y: 20, z: 40 }
        },
        link: {
            width: { default: 0.65, min: 0, max: 2, step: 0.05 },
            opacity: { default: 0.45, min: 0, max: 1, step: 0.01 }
        },
        node: {
            size: {
                chunk: { min: 0.5, max: 1.5 },  // Chunk узлы меньше
                other: { min: 1, max: 3 }       // Остальные пропорционально
            }
        }
    },
    
    stars: {
        default: 2000,
        min: 0,
        max: 4000,
        step: 100,
        radius: 400  // Уменьшенный радиус звезд
    },
    
    colors: {
        default: {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#292929'
        }
    },
    
    autoRotate: {
        speed: 0.00015,
        delay: 5000
    },
    
    api: {
        graph: 'http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200'
    }
};

# Создадим модуль ядра графа
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/modules/graph-core.js << 'EOF'
import config from './config.js';

export default class GraphCore {
    constructor(container) {
        this.container = container;
        this.graph = null;
        this.data = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        this.callbacks = {};
        this.isDragging = false;
        this.scale = config.graph.scale;
    }
    
    async loadData() {
        try {
            const res = await fetch(config.api.graph);
            const data = await res.json();
            this.processData(data);
            return true;
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Using mock data:', e);
            this.data = this.getMockData();
            return true;
        }
    }
    
    processData(data) {
        if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
        
        const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
            const group = node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
            const connections = data.edges.filter(e => 
                e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
            ).length;
            
            let val;
            if (group === 'Chunk') {
                val = config.graph.node.size.chunk.min + 
                      Math.min(connections, 10) / 10 * 
                      (config.graph.node.size.chunk.max - config.graph.node.size.chunk.min);
            } else {
                val = config.graph.node.size.other.min + 
                      Math.min(connections, 20) / 20 * 
                      (config.graph.node.size.other.max - config.graph.node.size.other.min);
            }
            
            return {
                id: String(node.id),
                name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                group: group,
                val: val * this.scale
            };
        });
        
        const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
            source: String(edge.source),
            target: String(edge.target),
            type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
        }));
        
        const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
            nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
            nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
        );
        
        this.data = { nodes, links: validLinks };
    }
    
    getMockData() {
        return {
            nodes: [
                { id: '1', name: 'Person 1', group: 'Person', val: 2 },
                { id: '2', name: 'Tech 1', group: 'Technology', val: 1.5 },
                { id: '3', name: 'Component 1', group: 'Component', val: 2.5 }
            ],
            links: [
                { source: '1', target: '2', type: 'uses' },
                { source: '2', target: '3', type: 'depends' }
            ]
        };
    }
    
    init() {
        this.graph = ForceGraph3D()(this.container)
            .graphData(this.data)
            .nodeId('id')
            .nodeLabel('name')
            .nodeColor(n => this.callbacks.getColor?.(n.group) || '#888')
            .nodeVal('val')
            .linkWidth(config.graph.link.width.default)
            .linkOpacity(config.graph.link.opacity.default)
            .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
            .onNodeHover(node => this.handleNodeHover(node))
            .onNodeClick(node => this.handleNodeClick(node))
            .onBackgroundClick(() => this.handleBackgroundClick());
        
        this.setupInteractionTracking();
        this.setCamera(config.graph.camera.initial);
    }
    
    setupInteractionTracking() {
        const canvas = this.graph.renderer().domElement;
        canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
            this.isDragging = true;
            this.lastInteraction = Date.now();
        });
        canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
            this.isDragging = false;
        });
        canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => {
            this.lastInteraction = Date.now();
        });
    }
    
    handleNodeHover(node) {
        if (!this.isDragging && !this.callbacks.isNodeSelected?.()) {
            this.callbacks.onNodeHover?.(node);
        }
    }
    
    handleNodeClick(node) {
        if (!this.isDragging) {
            this.callbacks.onNodeClick?.(node);
        }
    }
    
    handleBackgroundClick() {
        this.callbacks.onBackgroundClick?.();
    }
    
    setCamera(pos, lookAt, duration) {
        if (!this.graph) return;
        if (lookAt) {
            this.graph.cameraPosition(pos, lookAt, duration);
        } else {
            this.graph.cameraPosition(pos);
        }
    }
    
    highlight(nodeId, connectedIds) {
        if (!this.graph) return;
        
        this.graph.nodeColor(n => {
            if (n.id === nodeId || connectedIds?.has(n.id)) {
                return this.callbacks.getColor?.(n.group) || '#888';
            }
            return '#333';
        });
    }
    
    resetHighlight() {
        if (!this.graph) return;
        this.graph.nodeColor(n => this.callbacks.getColor?.(n.group) || '#888');
    }
    
    setCallbacks(callbacks) {
        this.callbacks = callbacks;
    }
    
    updateLinkWidth(val) {
        if (this.graph) this.graph.linkWidth(val);
    }
    
    updateLinkOpacity(val) {
        if (this.graph) this.graph.linkOpacity(val);
    }
    
    getCamera() {
        return this.graph?.camera();
    }
    
    getScene() {
        return this.graph?.scene();
    }
    
    getData() {
        return this.data;
    }
}
EOF

# Создадим модуль управления цветами
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/modules/colors.js << 'EOF'
import config from './config.js';

export default class ColorManager {
    constructor() {
        this.colors = { ...config.colors.default };
    }
    
    getColor(group) {
        return this.colors[group] || '#888';
    }
    
    getGroups() {
        return Object.keys(this.colors);
    }
    
    updateColor(group, color) {
        this.colors[group] = color;
    }
    
    randomize() {
        const groups = this.getGroups();
        groups.forEach(group => {
            this.colors[group] = this.getRandomBrightColor();
        });
    }
    
    getRandomBrightColor() {
        const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
        return `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 60%)`;
    }
    
    reset() {
        this.colors = { ...config.colors.default };
    }
    
    renderControls(containerId, onChange) {
        const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
        if (!container) return;
        
        let html = '';
        this.getGroups().forEach(group => {
            if (group !== 'unknown') {
                html += `
                    <div class="color-control">
                        <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                        <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${this.colors[group]}" 
                               data-group="${group}">
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        });
        
        container.innerHTML = html;
        
        // Attach events
        container.querySelectorAll('.color-input').forEach(input => {
            input.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                const group = e.target.dataset.group;
                this.updateColor(group, e.target.value);
                onChange?.();
            });
        });
    }
}
EOF

# Создадим модуль звезд
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/modules/stars.js << 'EOF'
import * as THREE from 'https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.module.js';

export default class StarsManager {
    constructor(scene, config) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.config = config;
        this.mesh = null;
    }
    
    create(count) {
        if (this.mesh) {
            this.scene.remove(this.mesh);
        }
        
        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        const colors = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.config.radius * 2;
            positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.config.radius * 2;
            positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.config.radius * 2;
            
            const brightness = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3;
            colors[i*3] = brightness;
            colors[i*3+1] = brightness * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2);
            colors[i*3+2] = brightness;
        }
        
        geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
        geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
        
        const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            size: 0.2,
            vertexColors: true,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.8,
            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
        });
        
        this.mesh = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
        this.scene.add(this.mesh);
        
        // Add fog
        this.scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a1a, 0.0006);
    }
    
    update(count) {
        this.create(count);
    }
}
EOF

# Создадим модуль панелей
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/modules/panels.js << 'EOF'
export default class PanelManager {
    constructor() {
        this.panels = {};
        this.nodePanelVisible = false;
    }
    
    init() {
        this.setupDraggable();
        this.setupCollapsible();
    }
    
    setupDraggable() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            let panel = header.parentElement;
            let isDragging = false;
            let offsetX, offsetY;
            
            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    isDragging = true;
                    offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                    offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDragging) {
                    panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                    panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                    panel.style.transform = 'none';
                    
                    if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        this.positionNodePanel();
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDragging = false;
            });
        });
    }
    
    setupCollapsible() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
                    header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                    
                    if (header.parentElement.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        if (!wasCollapsed) {
                            this.hideNodePanel();
                        } else {
                            setTimeout(() => this.positionNodePanel(), 300);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    }
    
    showNodePanel(node, connections) {
        const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
        const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
        const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
        
        panel.classList.add('visible');
        title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
        
        let html = `
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">ID</div>
                <div>${node.id}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>
                <div>${connections.length}</div>
            </div>
        `;
        
        if (connections.length > 0) {
            connections.forEach(conn => {
                html += `
                    <div class="connection-item" data-node="${conn.targetId}">
                        ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                    </div>
                `;
            });
        }
        
        content.innerHTML = html;
        this.nodePanelVisible = true;
        this.positionNodePanel();
    }
    
    hideNodePanel() {
        document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
        this.nodePanelVisible = false;
    }
    
    positionNodePanel() {
        const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
        const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
        
        if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel || !this.nodePanelVisible) return;
        
        const controlsRect = controlsPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
        const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsRect.height;
        
        nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
    }
    
    updateStats(data) {
        const groups = {};
        data.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
        
        let html = `
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                <span class="stat-value">${data.nodes.length}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                <span class="stat-value">${data.links.length}</span>
            </div>
        `;
        
        for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
            html += `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
        document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
    }
    
    showSearchResults(results, callback) {
        const container = document.getElementById('search-results');
        container.innerHTML = results.map(r => 
            `<button onclick="window.focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`
        ).join('');
    }
}
EOF
```
