```bash
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>UCH Knowledge Graph - Modular</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="css/blender.css">
    <style>
        /* Дополнительные стили для отступов */
        #search-panel {
            top: 270px;  /* Увеличил отступ, чтобы не налезало */
        }
        
        .stat-row:last-child {
            border-bottom: none;  /* Убрал линию у последней строки */
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="viewport"></div>
    
    <div id="stats-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>📊 STATISTICS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="stats-content">
            Loading...
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="search-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>🔍 SEARCH</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <input type="text" id="search-input" placeholder="Search nodes...">
            <div id="search-results"></div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="controls-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>🎮 CONTROLS</span>
            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">APPEARANCE</div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link width</span>
                        <span id="width-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.65</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.65">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>
                        <span id="opacity-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.45</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Stars count</span>
                        <span id="stars-value" style="color: #667eea;">2000</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="stars-slider" min="0" max="4000" step="100" value="2000">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button id="randomize-colors">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button id="reset-colors">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="close-btn" id="close-node-panel">✖</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
            Select a node to view details
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
        <button id="rotate-btn" class="icon-btn">
            <span>⏸️</span>
            <span>Stop</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <!-- Библиотеки -->
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>
    
    <!-- Модули (как ES modules) -->
    <script type="import">
        import config from './js/modules/config.js';
        import GraphCore from './js/modules/graph-core.js';
        import ColorManager from './js/modules/colors.js';
        import StarsManager from './js/modules/stars.js';
        import PanelManager from './js/modules/panels.js';
        
        // Глобальные переменные
        window.graphCore = null;
        window.colorManager = new ColorManager();
        window.panelManager = new PanelManager();
        window.starsManager = null;
        
        let rotationEnabled = true;
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        let fixedRadius = null;
        let rotationAngle = 0;
        let selectedNodeId = null;
        
        // Инициализация
        async function init() {
            panelManager.init();
            
            // Создаем граф
            window.graphCore = new GraphCore(document.getElementById('viewport'));
            
            // Устанавливаем колбэки
            graphCore.setCallbacks({
                getColor: (group) => colorManager.getColor(group),
                isNodeSelected: () => selectedNodeId !== null,
                onNodeHover: (node) => {
                    if (!selectedNodeId) highlightNode(node);
                },
                onNodeClick: (node) => {
                    selectedNodeId = node.id;
                    showNodeInfo(node);
                    highlightNode(node, true);
                    
                    const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                    if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                        controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                    }
                    
                    setTimeout(() => panelManager.positionNodePanel(), 300);
                },
                onBackgroundClick: () => {
                    if (selectedNodeId) {
                        panelManager.hideNodePanel();
                        selectedNodeId = null;
                        graphCore.resetHighlight();
                    }
                }
            });
            
            // Загружаем данные
            await graphCore.loadData();
            graphCore.init();
            
            // Инициализируем звезды
            const scene = graphCore.getScene();
            window.starsManager = new StarsManager(scene, config.stars);
            starsManager.create(config.stars.default);
            
            // Обновляем статистику
            panelManager.updateStats(graphCore.getData());
            
            // Инициализируем контролы цветов
            colorManager.renderControls('color-controls', () => {
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphCore.getData().nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                } else {
                    graphCore.resetHighlight();
                }
            });
            
            // Настраиваем обработчики
            setupEventListeners();
            
            // Запускаем авто-вращение
            setupAutoRotate();
        }
        
        function setupEventListeners() {
            // Слайдеры
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                graphCore.updateLinkWidth(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                graphCore.updateLinkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('stars-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                document.getElementById('stars-value').textContent = val;
                starsManager.update(val);
            });
            
            // Кнопки цветов
            document.getElementById('randomize-colors').addEventListener('click', () => {
                colorManager.randomize();
                colorManager.renderControls('color-controls');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphCore.getData().nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                } else {
                    graphCore.resetHighlight();
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('reset-colors').addEventListener('click', () => {
                colorManager.reset();
                colorManager.renderControls('color-controls');
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphCore.getData().nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                } else {
                    graphCore.resetHighlight();
                }
            });
            
            // Вращение
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
                btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? '<span>⏸️</span><span>Stop</span>' : '<span>▶️</span><span>Start</span>';
            });
            
            // Поиск
            document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
                if (!query) {
                    document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                    return;
                }
                
                const results = graphCore.getData().nodes
                    .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                    .slice(0, 5);
                
                panelManager.showSearchResults(results, (nodeId) => {
                    const node = graphCore.getData().nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
                    if (node) {
                        selectedNodeId = node.id;
                        showNodeInfo(node);
                        highlightNode(node, true);
                        graphCore.setCamera(config.graph.camera.focus, node, 800);
                    }
                });
            });
            
            // Закрытие панели узла
            document.getElementById('close-node-panel').addEventListener('click', () => {
                panelManager.hideNodePanel();
                selectedNodeId = null;
                graphCore.resetHighlight();
            });
        }
        
        function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
            if (!node) {
                graphCore.resetHighlight();
                return;
            }
            
            const nodeId = node.id;
            const data = graphCore.getData();
            
            const connectedLinks = data.links.filter(l => 
                (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
                (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
            );
            
            const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
            connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
                connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
            });
            
            graphCore.highlight(nodeId, connectedNodeIds);
        }
        
        function showNodeInfo(node) {
            const data = graphCore.getData();
            const connections = [];
            
            data.links.forEach(link => {
                const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
                const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
                
                if (sourceId === node.id) {
                    const target = data.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                        direction: '→',
                        targetId: targetId
                    });
                }
                if (targetId === node.id) {
                    const source = data.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                        direction: '←',
                        targetId: sourceId
                    });
                }
            });
            
            panelManager.showNodePanel(node, connections);
            
            // Добавляем обработчики для connection-item
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.querySelectorAll('.connection-item').forEach(item => {
                    item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const targetId = item.dataset.node;
                        const targetNode = data.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                        if (targetNode) {
                            selectedNodeId = targetId;
                            showNodeInfo(targetNode);
                            highlightNode(targetNode, true);
                            graphCore.setCamera(config.graph.camera.focus, targetNode, 800);
                        }
                    });
                });
            }, 100);
        }
        
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graphCore || !rotationEnabled) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                if (!graphCore.isDragging && now - lastInteraction > config.autoRotate.delay) {
                    const camera = graphCore.getCamera();
                    if (camera) {
                        if (!fixedRadius) {
                            fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                                camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                                camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                                camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                            );
                            rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                        }
                        
                        rotationAngle += deltaTime * config.autoRotate.speed;
                        camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                        camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                    }
                } else {
                    fixedRadius = null;
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                    lastInteraction = Date.now();
                    graphCore.isDragging = true;
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                    graphCore.isDragging = false;
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => {
                    lastInteraction = Date.now();
                });
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        // Экспортируем функцию для поиска
        window.focusNode = (nodeId) => {
            const node = graphCore.getData().nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (node) {
                selectedNodeId = node.id;
                showNodeInfo(node);
                highlightNode(node, true);
                graphCore.setCamera(config.graph.camera.focus, node, 800);
                
                const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                    controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                }
                
                setTimeout(() => panelManager.positionNodePanel(), 300);
            }
        };
        
        // Запуск
        init();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

```bash
        #bottom-panel { bottom: 20px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%); padding: 8px 20px; }

</head>
<body>
    <div id="viewport"></div>
    
    <div id="stats-panel" class="panel">STATISTICS<br>Loading...</div>
    <div id="search-panel" class="panel">SEARCH<br><input type="text" style="width:100%; margin-top:5px;"></div>
    <div id="controls-panel" class="panel">CONTROLS<br>Sliders will be here</div>
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">Connected | Updated: just now | ⏸️ Stop</div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        console.log('Starting test...');
        
        // Тестовые данные
        const testData = {
            nodes: [
                { id: '1', name: 'Node 1', group: 'Person' },
                { id: '2', name: 'Node 2', group: 'Technology' },
                { id: '3', name: 'Node 3', group: 'Component' }
            ],
            links: [
                { source: '1', target: '2', type: 'knows' },
                { source: '2', target: '3', type: 'uses' }
            ]
        };
        
        try {
            const graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
                .graphData(testData)
                .nodeColor(n => n.group === 'Person' ? '#3366ff' : '#33cc33')
                .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a');
                
            console.log('Graph created successfully');
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Error:', e);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```bash
                <div class="control-title">APPEARANCE</div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link width</span>
                        <span id="width-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.65</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.65">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>
                        <span id="opacity-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.45</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Stars count</span>
                        <span id="stars-value" style="color: #667eea;">2000</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="stars-slider" min="0" max="4000" step="100" value="2000">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button id="randomize-colors">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button id="reset-colors">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="close-btn" id="close-node-panel">✖</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
            Select a node to view details
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
        <button id="rotate-btn" class="icon-btn">
            <span>⏸️</span>
            <span>Stop</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        // Глобальные переменные
        let graph;
        let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        let colors = {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#292929'
        };
        let rotationEnabled = true;
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        let selectedNodeId = null;
        let fixedRadius = null;
        let rotationAngle = 0;
        let starsCount = 2000;
        let starsMesh = null;
        let isDraggingGraph = false;
        
        // Конфигурация
        const config = {
            graph: {
                scale: 0.5,
                camera: {
                    initial: { x: 110, y: 60, z: 150 },
                    focus: { x: 40, y: 20, z: 40 }
                }
            },
            autoRotate: {
                speed: 0.00015,
                delay: 5000
            }
        };
        
        // Загрузка данных
        fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                console.log('Data loaded:', data);
                if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
                
                const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                    const group = node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
                    const connections = data.edges.filter(e => 
                        e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
                    ).length;
                    
                    let val;
                    if (group === 'Chunk') {
                        val = 0.5 + Math.min(connections, 10) / 20;
                    } else {
                        val = 1 + Math.min(connections, 20) / 10;
                    }
                    
                    return {
                        id: String(node.id),
                        name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                        group: group,
                        val: val
                    };
                });
                
                const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
                    source: String(edge.source),
                    target: String(edge.target),
                    type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
                }));
                
                const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                    nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
                );
                
                graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
                initGraph();
                updateStats();
                initColorControls();
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error loading data:', error);
                // Тестовые данные
                graphData = {
                    nodes: [
                        { id: '1', name: 'Person 1', group: 'Person', val: 1.5 },
                        { id: '2', name: 'Tech 1', group: 'Technology', val: 1.2 },
                        { id: '3', name: 'Component 1', group: 'Component', val: 1.3 }
                    ],
                    links: [
                        { source: '1', target: '2', type: 'uses' },
                        { source: '2', target: '3', type: 'depends' }
                    ]
                };
                initGraph();
                updateStats();
                initColorControls();
            });
        
        function initGraph() {
            const oldCanvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (oldCanvas) oldCanvas.remove();
            
            graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
                .graphData(graphData)
                .nodeId('id')
                .nodeLabel('name')
                .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                .nodeVal('val')
                .linkWidth(0.65)
                .linkOpacity(0.45)
                .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
                .onNodeHover(node => {
                    document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
                    if (!selectedNodeId && !isDraggingGraph) highlightNode(node);
                })
                .onNodeClick(node => {
                    if (!isDraggingGraph) {
                        selectedNodeId = node.id;
                        showNodeInfo(node);
                        highlightNode(node, true);
                        
                        const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                        if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                            controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                        }
                        
                        setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                    }
                })
                .onBackgroundClick(() => {
                    if (selectedNodeId) {
                        closeNodePanel();
                    }
                });
            
            // Отслеживание перетаскивания
            const canvas = graph.renderer().domElement;
            canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                isDraggingGraph = true;
                lastInteraction = Date.now();
            });
            canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDraggingGraph = false;
            });
            canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (e.buttons === 1) lastInteraction = Date.now();
            });
            canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => {
                lastInteraction = Date.now();
            });
            
            graph.cameraPosition(config.graph.camera.initial);
            addStars(starsCount);
            setupControls();
            setupAutoRotate();
        }
        
        function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
            if (!graph) return;
            
            if (node) {
                const nodeId = node.id;
                const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
                    (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
                    (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
                );
                
                const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
                connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
                    connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
                });
                
                graph.nodeColor(n => {
                    if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                        return colors[n.group] || '#888';
                    }
                    return '#333';
                });
                
                graph.linkColor(l => {
                    if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
                    return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                });
                
                graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : 0.65);
            } else if (!permanent) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                     .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
                     .linkWidth(0.65);
            }
        }
        
        function showNodeInfo(node) {
            const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
            const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
            
            panel.classList.add('visible');
            title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
            
            const connections = [];
            graphData.links.forEach(link => {
                const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
                const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
                
                if (sourceId === node.id) {
                    const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                        direction: '→',
                        targetId: targetId
                    });
                }
                if (targetId === node.id) {
                    const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                        direction: '←',
                        targetId: sourceId
                    });
                }
            });
            
            let html = `
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                    <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">ID</div>
                    <div>${node.id}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="node-property">
                    <div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>
                    <div>${connections.length}</div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            if (connections.length > 0) {
                connections.forEach(conn => {
                    html += `
                        <div class="connection-item" data-node="${conn.targetId}">
                            ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
            }
            
            content.innerHTML = html;
            
            // Добавляем обработчики для connection-item
            document.querySelectorAll('.connection-item').forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const targetId = item.dataset.node;
                    const targetNode = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    if (targetNode) {
                        selectedNodeId = targetId;
                        showNodeInfo(targetNode);
                        highlightNode(targetNode, true);
                        
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        if (camera) {
                            const distance = 40;
                            graph.cameraPosition(
                                { x: distance, y: distance/2, z: distance },
                                targetNode,
                                800
                            );
                        }
                    }
                });
            });
        }
        
        function closeNodePanel() {
            document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
            selectedNodeId = null;
            highlightNode(null);
        }
        
        function positionNodePanel() {
            const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
            const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            
            if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel || !nodePanel.classList.contains('visible')) return;
            
            const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsPanel.offsetHeight;
            nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
        }
        
        function updateStats() {
            const groups = {};
            graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
            
            let html = `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.nodes.length}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${graphData.links.length}</span>
                </div>
            `;
            
            for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
                html += `
                    <div class="stat-row">
                        <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                        <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
            document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
        }
        
        function initColorControls() {
            const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
            if (!container) return;
            
            const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
            let html = '';
            
            groups.forEach(group => {
                if (group !== 'unknown') {
                    html += `
                        <div class="color-control">
                            <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                            <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${colors[group] || '#888'}" 
                                   data-group="${group}">
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
            });
            
            container.innerHTML = html;
            
            container.querySelectorAll('.color-input').forEach(input => {
                input.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                    const group = e.target.dataset.group;
                    colors[group] = e.target.value;
                    if (selectedNodeId) {
                        const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                        if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                    } else {
                        graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                    }
                });
            });
        }
        
        function getRandomBrightColor() {
            const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
            return `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 60%)`;
        }
        
        function setupControls() {
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
            });
            
            document.getElementById('stars-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                document.getElementById('stars-value').textContent = val;
                starsCount = val;
                updateStars(val);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
                btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? '<span>⏸️</span><span>Stop</span>' : '<span>▶️</span><span>Start</span>';
                
                if (rotationEnabled) {
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    if (camera) {
                        fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                            camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                            camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                            camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                        );
                        rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('randomize-colors').addEventListener('click', () => {
                const groups = Object.keys(colors);
                groups.forEach(group => {
                    colors[group] = getRandomBrightColor();
                });
                initColorControls();
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                } else {
                    graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('reset-colors').addEventListener('click', () => {
                colors = {
                    'Person': '#3366ff',
                    'Technology': '#33cc33',
                    'Component': '#ff9933',
                    'Decision': '#cc33ff',
                    'Chunk': '#292929'
                };
                initColorControls();
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                    if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
                } else {
                    graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
                if (!query) {
                    document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                    return;
                }
                
                const results = graphData.nodes
                    .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                    .slice(0, 5);
                
                const container = document.getElementById('search-results');
                container.innerHTML = results.map(r => 
                    `<button onclick="window.focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`
                ).join('');
            });
            
            document.getElementById('close-node-panel').addEventListener('click', closeNodePanel);
        }
        
        window.focusNode = (nodeId) => {
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (node) {
                selectedNodeId = node.id;
                showNodeInfo(node);
                highlightNode(node, true);
                
                const camera = graph.camera();
                if (camera) {
                    const distance = 40;
                    graph.cameraPosition(
                        { x: distance, y: distance/2, z: distance },
                        node,
                        800
                    );
                }
                
                const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                    controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                }
                
                setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
            }
        };
        
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graph || !rotationEnabled) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                if (!isDraggingGraph && now - lastInteraction > config.autoRotate.delay) {
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    if (camera) {
                        if (!fixedRadius) {
                            fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                                camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                                camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                                camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                            );
                            rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                        }
                        
                        rotationAngle += deltaTime * config.autoRotate.speed;
                        camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                        camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                    }
                } else {
                    fixedRadius = null;
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        function addStars(count) {
            const scene = graph.scene();
            if (!scene) return;
            
            if (starsMesh) {
                scene.remove(starsMesh);
            }
            
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            const colors_array = new Float32Array(count * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
                
                const brightness = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3;
                colors_array[i*3] = brightness;
                colors_array[i*3+1] = brightness * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2);
                colors_array[i*3+2] = brightness;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors_array, 3));
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
                size: 0.2,
                vertexColors: true,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.8,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            starsMesh = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            scene.add(starsMesh);
            
            scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a1a, 0.0006);
        }
        
        function updateStars(count) {
            addStars(count);
        }
        
        // Draggable panels
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            let panel = header.parentElement;
            let isDragging = false;
            let offsetX, offsetY;
            
            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    isDragging = true;
                    offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                    offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDragging) {
                    panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                    panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                    
                    if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDragging = false;
            });
        });
        
        // Collapsible panels
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
                    header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                    
                    if (header.parentElement.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        if (!wasCollapsed) {
                            closeNodePanel();
                        } else {
                            setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
        
        window.addEventListener('resize', positionNodePanel);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Ты абсолютно прав! Нужна модульная структура. Просто в предыдущей версии были проблемы с импортами. Давай сделаем правильно, с обычными script-тегами вместо ES modules.

**ПЕРВАЯ КОМАНДА:**
```bash

            focus: { x: 40, y: 20, z: 40 }
        },
        link: {

# 2. Утилиты
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/utils.js << 'EOF'
// Утилиты
window.Utils = {
    getRandomBrightColor: function() {
        const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
        return `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 60%)`;
    },
    
    formatTime: function(date) {
        return date.toLocaleTimeString();
    },
    
    getNodeSize: function(group, connections) {
        if (group === 'Chunk') {
            return 0.5 + Math.min(connections, 10) / 20;
        } else {
            return 1 + Math.min(connections, 20) / 10;
        }
    },
    
    debounce: function(func, wait) {
        let timeout;
        return function executedFunction(...args) {
            const later = () => {
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                func(...args);
            };
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
        };
    }
};
EOF

# 3. Менеджер цветов
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/colorManager.js << 'EOF'
// Управление цветами
window.ColorManager = {
    colors: { ...window.CONFIG.colors },
    
    getColor: function(group) {
        return this.colors[group] || '#888';
    },
    
    getGroups: function() {
        return Object.keys(this.colors);
    },
    
    updateColor: function(group, color) {
        this.colors[group] = color;
        if (window.GraphCore && window.GraphCore.graph) {
            window.GraphCore.graph.nodeColor(n => this.colors[n.group] || '#888');
        }
    },
    
    randomize: function() {
        const groups = this.getGroups();
        groups.forEach(group => {
            this.colors[group] = Utils.getRandomBrightColor();
        });
        this.renderControls();
        if (window.GraphCore && window.GraphCore.graph) {
            window.GraphCore.graph.nodeColor(n => this.colors[n.group] || '#888');
        }
    },
    
    reset: function() {
        this.colors = { ...window.CONFIG.colors };
        this.renderControls();
        if (window.GraphCore && window.GraphCore.graph) {
            window.GraphCore.graph.nodeColor(n => this.colors[n.group] || '#888');
        }
    },
    
    renderControls: function() {
        const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
        if (!container) return;
        
        let html = '';
        this.getGroups().forEach(group => {
            if (group !== 'unknown') {
                html += `
                    <div class="color-control">
                        <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                        <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${this.colors[group]}" 
                               data-group="${group}">
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        });
        
        container.innerHTML = html;
        
        container.querySelectorAll('.color-input').forEach(input => {
            input.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                const group = e.target.dataset.group;
                this.updateColor(group, e.target.value);
                if (window.GraphCore && window.GraphCore.selectedNodeId) {
                    window.GraphCore.highlightSelected();
                }
            });
        });
    }
};
EOF

# 4. Ядро графа
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/graphCore.js << 'EOF'
// Ядро графа
window.GraphCore = {
    graph: null,
    data: { nodes: [], links: [] },
    selectedNodeId: null,
    isDragging: false,
    lastInteraction: Date.now(),
    
    init: function(container) {
        this.graph = ForceGraph3D()(container)
            .graphData(this.data)
            .nodeId('id')
            .nodeLabel('name')
            .nodeColor(n => ColorManager.getColor(n.group))
            .nodeVal('val')
            .linkWidth(CONFIG.graph.link.width.default)
            .linkOpacity(CONFIG.graph.link.opacity.default)
            .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
            .onNodeHover(node => this.onNodeHover(node))
            .onNodeClick(node => this.onNodeClick(node))
            .onBackgroundClick(() => this.onBackgroundClick());
        
        this.setupInteractionTracking();
        this.graph.cameraPosition(CONFIG.graph.camera.initial);
    },
    
    setupInteractionTracking: function() {
        const canvas = this.graph.renderer().domElement;
        canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
            this.isDragging = true;
            this.lastInteraction = Date.now();
        });
        canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
            this.isDragging = false;
        });
        canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if (e.buttons === 1) this.lastInteraction = Date.now();
        });
        canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => {
            this.lastInteraction = Date.now();
        });
    },
    
    onNodeHover: function(node) {
        document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
        if (!this.selectedNodeId && !this.isDragging) {
            this.highlightNode(node);
        }
    },
    
    onNodeClick: function(node) {
        if (!this.isDragging) {
            this.selectedNodeId = node.id;
            PanelManager.showNodeInfo(node);
            this.highlightNode(node, true);
            
            const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
            if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
            }
            
            setTimeout(() => PanelManager.positionNodePanel(), 300);
        }
    },
    
    onBackgroundClick: function() {
        if (this.selectedNodeId) {
            PanelManager.hideNodePanel();
            this.selectedNodeId = null;
            this.resetHighlight();
        }
    },
    
    highlightNode: function(node, permanent = false) {
        if (!node) {
            this.resetHighlight();
            return;
        }
        
        const nodeId = node.id;
        const connectedLinks = this.data.links.filter(l => 
            (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
            (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
        );
        
        const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
        connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
            connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
            connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
        });
        
        this.graph.nodeColor(n => {
            if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                return ColorManager.getColor(n.group);
            }
            return '#333';
        });
        
        this.graph.linkColor(l => {
            if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
            return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
        });
        
        this.graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : CONFIG.graph.link.width.default);
    },
    
    highlightSelected: function() {
        if (this.selectedNodeId) {
            const node = this.data.nodes.find(n => n.id === this.selectedNodeId);
            if (node) this.highlightNode(node, true);
        }
    },
    
    resetHighlight: function() {
        this.graph.nodeColor(n => ColorManager.getColor(n.group))
             .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
             .linkWidth(CONFIG.graph.link.width.default);
    },
    
    loadData: async function() {
        try {
            const res = await fetch(CONFIG.api.graph);
            const data = await res.json();
            this.processData(data);
            return true;
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Using mock data:', e);
            this.data = {
                nodes: [
                    { id: '1', name: 'Person 1', group: 'Person', val: 1.5 },
                    { id: '2', name: 'Tech 1', group: 'Technology', val: 1.2 },
                    { id: '3', name: 'Component 1', group: 'Component', val: 1.3 }
                ],
                links: [
                    { source: '1', target: '2', type: 'uses' },
                    { source: '2', target: '3', type: 'depends' }
                ]
            };
            return true;
        }
    },
    
    processData: function(data) {
        if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
        
        const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
            const group = node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
            const connections = data.edges.filter(e => 
                e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
            ).length;
            
            return {
                id: String(node.id),
                name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                group: group,
                val: Utils.getNodeSize(group, connections)
            };
        });
        
        const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
            source: String(edge.source),
            target: String(edge.target),
            type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
        }));
        
        const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
            nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
            nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
        );
        
        this.data = { nodes, links: validLinks };
    },
    
    updateLinkWidth: function(val) {
        if (this.graph) this.graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
    },
    
    updateLinkOpacity: function(val) {
        if (this.graph) this.graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
    },
    
    focusNode: function(nodeId) {
        const node = this.data.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
        if (node) {
            this.selectedNodeId = node.id;
            PanelManager.showNodeInfo(node);
            this.highlightNode(node, true);
            
            const distance = 40;
            this.graph.cameraPosition(
                { x: distance, y: distance/2, z: distance },
                node,
                800
            );
            
            const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
            if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
            }
            
            setTimeout(() => PanelManager.positionNodePanel(), 300);
        }
    },
    
    getCamera: function() {
        return this.graph?.camera();
    },
    
    getScene: function() {
        return this.graph?.scene();
    }
};
EOF

cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/panelManager.js << 'EOF'
// Управление панелями
window.PanelManager = {
    init: function() {
        this.setupDraggable();
        this.setupCollapsible();
    },
    
    setupDraggable: function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            let panel = header.parentElement;
            let isDragging = false;
            let offsetX, offsetY;
            
            header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    isDragging = true;
                    offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                    offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDragging) {
                    panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                    panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                    
                    if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        setTimeout(() => this.positionNodePanel(), 50);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDragging = false;
            });
        });
    },
    
    setupCollapsible: function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow')) {
                    const wasCollapsed = header.classList.contains('collapsed');
                    header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                    
                    if (header.parentElement.id === 'controls-panel') {
                        if (!wasCollapsed) {
                            this.hideNodePanel();
                        } else {
                            setTimeout(() => this.positionNodePanel(), 300);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    },
    
    showNodeInfo: function(node) {
        const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
        const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
        const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
        
        panel.classList.add('visible');
        title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
        
        const connections = [];
        GraphCore.data.links.forEach(link => {
            const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
            const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
            
            if (sourceId === node.id) {
                const target = GraphCore.data.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                connections.push({
                    type: link.type || 'related',
                    with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                    direction: '→',
                    targetId: targetId
                });
            }
            if (targetId === node.id) {
                const source = GraphCore.data.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                connections.push({
                    type: link.type || 'related',
                    with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                    direction: '←',
                    targetId: sourceId
                });
            }
        });
        
        let html = `
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">ID</div>
                <div>${node.id}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>
                <div>${connections.length}</div>
            </div>
        `;
        
        if (connections.length > 0) {
            connections.forEach(conn => {
                html += `
                    <div class="connection-item" data-node="${conn.targetId}">
                        ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                    </div>
                `;
            });
        }
        
        content.innerHTML = html;
        
        // Добавляем обработчики для connection-item
        document.querySelectorAll('.connection-item').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const targetId = item.dataset.node;
                GraphCore.focusNode(targetId);
            });
        });
    },
    
    hideNodePanel: function() {
        document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
        GraphCore.selectedNodeId = null;
        GraphCore.resetHighlight();
    },
    
    positionNodePanel: function() {
        const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
        const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
        
        if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel || !nodePanel.classList.contains('visible')) return;
        
        const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsPanel.offsetHeight;
        nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
    },
    
    updateStats: function() {
        const groups = {};
        GraphCore.data.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
        
        let html = `
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                <span class="stat-value">${GraphCore.data.nodes.length}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                <span class="stat-value">${GraphCore.data.links.length}</span>
            </div>
        `;
        
        for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
            html += `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
        document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = Utils.formatTime(new Date());
    },
    
    showSearchResults: function(results) {
        const container = document.getElementById('search-results');
        container.innerHTML = results.map(r => 
            `<button onclick="GraphCore.focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`
        ).join('');
    }
};
EOF

cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public/js/starsManager.js << 'EOF'
// Управление звездами
window.StarsManager = {
    mesh: null,
    count: CONFIG.stars.default,
    
    init: function(scene) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.create(this.count);
    },
    
    create: function(count) {
        if (this.mesh) {
            this.scene.remove(this.mesh);
        }
        
        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        const colors = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * CONFIG.stars.radius * 2;
            positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * CONFIG.stars.radius * 2;
            positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * CONFIG.stars.radius * 2;
            
            const brightness = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3;
            colors[i*3] = brightness;
            colors[i*3+1] = brightness * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2);
            colors[i*3+2] = brightness;
        }
        
        geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
        geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
        
        const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            size: 0.2,
            vertexColors: true,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.8,
            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
        });
        
        this.mesh = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
        this.scene.add(this.mesh);
        this.scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a1a, 0.0006);
    },
    
    update: function(count) {
        this.count = count;
        this.create(count);
    }
};
EOF

<body>

        <div class="panel-header">

            <span class="arrow">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">

                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>

                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>

                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Stars count</span>
                        <span id="stars-value" style="color: #667eea;">2000</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="stars-slider" min="0" max="4000" step="100" value="2000">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button id="randomize-colors">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button id="reset-colors">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>

    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">

            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="close-btn" id="close-node-panel">✖</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
            Select a node to view details
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
        <button id="rotate-btn" class="icon-btn">
            <span>⏸️</span>
            <span>Stop</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <!-- Libraries -->
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>
    
    <!-- Modules -->
    <script src="js/config.js"></script>
    <script src="js/utils.js"></script>
    <script src="js/colorManager.js"></script>
    <script src="js/panelManager.js"></script>
    <script src="js/graphCore.js"></script>
    <script src="js/starsManager.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        // Инициализация
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
            console.log('Initializing modules...');
            
            // Инициализируем менеджер панелей
            PanelManager.init();
            
            // Загружаем данные
            await GraphCore.loadData();
            
            // Инициализируем граф
            GraphCore.init(document.getElementById('viewport'));
            
            // Инициализируем звезды
            StarsManager.init(GraphCore.getScene());
            
            // Обновляем статистику
            PanelManager.updateStats();
            
            // Рендерим контролы цветов
            ColorManager.renderControls();
            
            // Настройка авто-вращения
            let rotationEnabled = true;
            let fixedRadius = null;
            let rotationAngle = 0;
            
            function setupAutoRotate() {
                let lastTime = Date.now();
                
                function rotateCamera() {
                    if (!GraphCore.graph || !rotationEnabled) return;
                    
                    const now = Date.now();
                    const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                    lastTime = now;
                    
                    if (!GraphCore.isDragging && now - GraphCore.lastInteraction > CONFIG.autoRotate.delay) {
                        const camera = GraphCore.getCamera();
                        if (camera) {
                            if (!fixedRadius) {
                                fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                                    camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                                    camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                                    camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                                );
                                rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                            }
                            
                            rotationAngle += deltaTime * CONFIG.autoRotate.speed;
                            camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                            camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                            camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                        }
                    } else {
                        fixedRadius = null;
                    }
                    
                    requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
                }
                
                rotateCamera();
            }
            
            setupAutoRotate();
            
            // Настройка обработчиков
            document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
                GraphCore.updateLinkWidth(val);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
                GraphCore.updateLinkOpacity(val);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('stars-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                document.getElementById('stars-value').textContent = val;
                StarsManager.update(val);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
                const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
                btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? '<span>⏸️</span><span>Stop</span>' : '<span>▶️</span><span>Start</span>';
                
                if (rotationEnabled) {
                    const camera = GraphCore.getCamera();
                    if (camera) {
                        fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                            camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                            camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                            camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                        );
                        rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                    }
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('randomize-colors').addEventListener('click', () => {
                ColorManager.randomize();
                if (GraphCore.selectedNodeId) {
                    GraphCore.highlightSelected();
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('reset-colors').addEventListener('click', () => {
                ColorManager.reset();
                if (GraphCore.selectedNodeId) {
                    GraphCore.highlightSelected();
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('close-node-panel').addEventListener('click', () => {
                PanelManager.hideNodePanel();
            });
            
            document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', Utils.debounce((e) => {
                const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
                if (!query) {
                    document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                    return;
                }
                
                const results = GraphCore.data.nodes
                    .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                    .slice(0, 5);
                
                PanelManager.showSearchResults(results);
            }, 300));
            
            // Обновляем статистику каждые 5 секунд
            setInterval(() => PanelManager.updateStats(), 5000);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```
